------------------------------------------------------------
Total distinct schedules (rearrangements): 904
NOTE: a schedule may list several valid mappings for the SAME
sub-expression -- both DIRECT binds (e.g. cblas_sgemm, cblas_ssymm)
and TILING/fix maps (e.g. the 1-D cblas_sdot order-reduced onto the
j-contraction). Mosaic's search returns ALL of them; with no
autoscheduler (paper, future work) the default pick is the first
direct bind. This driver shows the full set, pre-selection.
